    17→
    18→  def update
    19→    if @sandbox.update(params.require(:sandbox).permit(:name))
    20→      redirect_to @sandbox, notice: "Sandbox renamed to #{@sandbox.name}."
    21→    else
    22→      @sandbox_snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
    23→                             .select { |s| s[:source_sandbox] == @sandbox.name }
    24→      @routes = @sandbox.routes.order(:created_at)
    25→      @btrfs = BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
    26→      flash.now[:alert] = @sandbox.errors.full_messages.join(", ")
    27→      render :show, status: :unprocessable_entity
    28→    end
    29→  end
    30→
    31→  def create
    32→    authorize Sandbox
    33→
    34→    from_snapshot_name = params[:snapshot].presence
    35→
    36→    image = if from_snapshot_name.present?
    37→      snap = Snapshot.find_by(user: Current.user, name: from_snapshot_name)
    38→      snap&.docker_image || "sc-snap-#{Current.user.name}:#{from_snapshot_name}"
    39→    else
    40→      params[:image].presence || SandboxManager::DEFAULT_IMAGE
    41→    end
    42→
    43→    # Build sandbox record
    44→    # Note: temporary sandboxes can only be created via CLI
    45→    sandbox = Current.user.sandboxes.build(
    46→      name: params.require(:name),
    47→      status: "pending",
    48→      image: image,
    49→      persistent_volume: params[:persistent] == "1",
    50→      mount_home: params[:mount_home] == "1",
    51→      data_path: params[:data_path].presence,
    52→      tailscale: params[:tailscale] == "1",

    54→      vnc_geometry: Sandbox::VNC_GEOMETRIES.include?(params[:vnc_geometry]) ? params[:vnc_geometry] : "1280x900",
    55→      vnc_depth: Sandbox::VNC_DEPTHS.include?(params[:vnc_depth].to_i) ? params[:vnc_depth].to_i : 24,
    56→      temporary: false
    57→    )
    58→
    59→    if sandbox.persistent_volume
    60→      sandbox.volume_path = "#{SandboxManager::DATA_DIR}/sandboxes/#{sandbox.full_name}/vol"
    61→    end
    62→
    63→    if sandbox.save
    64→      # Enqueue async job
    65→      SandboxProvisionJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: sandbox.id)
    66→
    67→      # Optimistic redirect - dashboard will update via Turbo
    68→      redirect_to root_path, notice: "Creating sandcastle #{sandbox.name}..."
    69→    else
    70→      @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
    71→      flash.now[:alert] = "Failed to create sandbox: #{sandbox.errors.full_messages.join(', ')}"
    72→      render :new, status: :unprocessable_entity
    73→    end
    74→  rescue => e

    80→  def metrics
    81→    points = @sandbox.container_metrics.recent
    82→      .map { |m| { t: m.recorded_at.to_i, cpu: m.cpu_percent, mem: m.memory_mb.round(0) } }
    83→
    84→    # Append current live stats so graphs render instantly on page load
    85→    if @sandbox.status == "running" && @sandbox.container_id.present?
    86→      full = Rails.cache.fetch("sandbox_stats_full:#{@sandbox.id}", expires_in: 4.seconds) do
    87→        raw = Docker::Container.get(@sandbox.container_id).stats(stream: false)
    88→        next nil if raw.blank?
    89→        {
    90→          cpu_percent: StatsCalculator.cpu_percent(raw),
    91→          memory_mb: StatsCalculator.memory_mb(raw),
    92→          memory_limit_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "limit") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    93→          net_rx: 0, net_tx: 0, disk_read: 0, disk_write: 0, pids: 0
    94→        }
    95→      rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
    96→        nil
    97→      end
    98→      points << { t: Time.current.to_i, cpu: full[:cpu_percent], mem: full[:memory_mb].round(0) } if full
    99→    end
   100→
   101→    render json: points
   102→  end
   103→
   104→  def logs
   105→    tail = (params[:tail] || 200).to_i.clamp(1, 5000)
   106→    @tail = tail
   107→    @logs = SandboxManager.new.logs(sandbox: @sandbox, tail: tail, timestamps: true)
   108→  rescue SandboxManager::Error => e
   109→    @logs = nil
   110→    @log_error = e.message
   111→  end
   112→
   113→  def destroy
   114→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
   115→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
   116→      return
   117→    end
   118→
   119→    user = Current.user
   120→    archive = user.effective_archive_retention_days > 0
   121→
   122→    @sandbox.start_job("destroying")
   123→    SandboxDestroyJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id, archive: archive)
   124→
   125→    notice = archive ? "Archiving sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." : "Destroying sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..."
   126→    respond_to do |format|
   127→      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: notice }
   128→      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
   129→    end
   130→  end
   131→
   132→  def archive_restore
   133→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
   134→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
   135→      return
   136→    end
   137→
   138→    @sandbox.start_job("restoring")
   139→    SandboxRestoreJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
   140→    redirect_to root_path, notice: "Restoring sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..."
   141→  end
   142→
   143→  def start
   144→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
   145→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
   146→      return
   147→    end
   148→
   149→    @sandbox.start_job("starting")
   150→    SandboxStartJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
   151→
   152→    respond_to do |format|
   153→      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Starting sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
   154→      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
   155→    end
   156→  end
   157→
   158→  def stop
   159→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
   160→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
   161→      return
   162→    end
   163→
   164→    @sandbox.start_job("stopping")
   165→    SandboxStopJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
   166→
   167→    respond_to do |format|
   168→      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Stopping sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
   169→      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
   170→    end
   171→  end
   172→
   173→  def retry
   174→    return unless @sandbox.job_failed?
   175→
   176→    @sandbox.update!(job_error: nil)
   177→
   178→    case @sandbox.status
   179→    when "destroyed", "pending"
   180→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Cannot retry creation. Please create a new sandbox."
   181→    when "stopped"
   182→      SandboxStartJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
   183→      redirect_to root_path, notice: "Retrying start..."
   184→    when "running"
   185→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Sandbox is already running"
   186→    end
   187→  end
   188→
   189→  private
   190→
   191→  def set_sandbox
   192→    @sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
   193→    authorize @sandbox
   194→  end
   195→
   196→  def set_archived_sandbox
   197→    @sandbox = Current.user.sandboxes.archived.find(params[:id])
   198→    authorize @sandbox, :archive_restore?
   199→  end
   200→end
   201→

   131→      logs = SandboxManager.new.logs(sandbox: @sandbox, tail: tail, timestamps: params[:timestamps] == "true")
   132→      render json: { logs: logs }
   133→    end
   134→
   135→    def connect
   136→      # Allow pending sandboxes to return connect info (they may still be provisioning)
   137→      info = SandboxManager.new.connect_info(sandbox: @sandbox)
   138→      render json: info
   139→    rescue SandboxManager::Error => e
   140→      # If sandbox isn't ready yet, return helpful error
   141→      if @sandbox.status == "pending"
   142→        render json: {
   143→          error: "Sandbox is still being provisioned",
   144→          status: @sandbox.status,
   145→          job_status: @sandbox.job_status
   146→        }, status: :accepted
   147→      else
   148→        raise
   149→      end
   150→    end
   151→
   152→    def snapshot
   153→      layers = params[:layers].present? ? Array(params[:layers]) : nil
   154→      snap = SandboxManager.new.create_snapshot(
   155→        sandbox: @sandbox,
   156→        name: params[:name],
   157→        label: params[:label],
   158→        layers: layers,
   159→        data_subdir: params[:data_subdir]
   160→      )
   161→      render json: SandboxManager.new.snapshot_json(snap), status: :created
   162→    end
   163→
   164→    def restore
   165→      layers = params[:layers].present? ? Array(params[:layers]) : nil
   166→      SandboxManager.new.restore(
   167→        sandbox: @sandbox,
   168→        snapshot_name: params.require(:snapshot),
   169→        layers: layers
   170→      )
   171→      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox.reload)
   172→    end
   173→
   174→    def archive_restore
   175→      @sandbox.start_job("restoring")
   176→      SandboxRestoreJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
   177→      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox.reload), status: :accepted
   178→    end
   179→
   180→    def purge
   181→      @sandbox.start_job("destroying")
   182→      SandboxDestroyJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id, archive: false)
   183→      render json: { status: "accepted" }, status: :accepted
   184→    end
   185→
   186→    def tailscale_connect
   187→      TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: @sandbox)
   188→      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox.reload)
   189→    end
   190→
   191→    def tailscale_disconnect
   192→      TailscaleManager.new.disconnect_sandbox(sandbox: @sandbox)
   193→      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox.reload)
   194→    end
   195→
   196→    private
   197→
   198→    def set_sandbox
   199→      @sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
   200→      authorize @sandbox
   201→    rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
   202→      # Check if sandbox exists but is out of scope (destroyed or wrong user)
   203→      sandbox = Sandbox.find_by(id: params[:id])
   204→      if sandbox.nil?
   205→        raise ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound, "Sandbox with ID #{params[:id]} not found"
   206→      elsif sandbox.status.in?(%w[destroyed archived])
   207→        raise ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound, "Sandbox with ID #{params[:id]} has been #{sandbox.status}"
   208→      elsif sandbox.user_id != current_user.id
   209→        raise Pundit::NotAuthorizedError, "You don't have access to this sandbox"
   210→      else
   211→        # Sandbox exists and belongs to user, but not in policy scope - this shouldn't happen
   212→        Rails.logger.error("Sandbox #{params[:id]} exists (status: #{sandbox.status}, user: #{sandbox.user_id}) but not in policy_scope for user #{current_user.id}")
   213→        raise ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound, "Sandbox with ID #{params[:id]} is not accessible (status: #{sandbox.status})"
   214→      end
   215→    end
   216→
   217→    def set_archived_sandbox
   218→      @sandbox = if current_user.admin?
   219→        Sandbox.archived.find(params[:id])
   220→      else
   221→        current_user.sandboxes.archived.find(params[:id])
   222→      end
   223→      authorize @sandbox, action_name == "purge" ? :purge? : :archive_restore?
   224→    rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
   225→      raise ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound, "Archived sandbox with ID #{params[:id]} not found"
   226→    end
   227→
   228→    def sandbox_json(sandbox)
   229→      json = {
   230→        id: sandbox.id,
   231→        name: sandbox.name,
   232→        full_name: sandbox.full_name,
   233→        status: sandbox.status,
   234→        image: sandbox.image,
   235→        persistent_volume: sandbox.persistent_volume,
   236→        mount_home: sandbox.mount_home,
   237→        data_path: sandbox.data_path,
   238→        temporary: sandbox.temporary,
   239→        tailscale: sandbox.tailscale,
   240→        vnc_enabled: sandbox.vnc_enabled,
   241→        vnc_geometry: sandbox.vnc_geometry,
   242→        vnc_depth: sandbox.vnc_depth,
   243→        routes: sandbox.routes.map { |r| { id: r.id, domain: r.domain, port: r.port, url: r.url } },
   244→        created_at: sandbox.created_at,
   245→        archived_at: sandbox.archived_at,
   246→        connect_command: sandbox.connect_command,
   247→        job_status: sandbox.job_status,
   248→        job_error: sandbox.job_error
   249→      }
   250→
   251→      if sandbox.tailscale?
   252→        json[:tailscale_ip] = TailscaleManager.new.sandbox_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox)
   253→      end
   254→
   255→      json
   256→    end
   257→  end
   258→end
   259→
```

    68→
    69→If no name is provided, creates a temporary sandbox with an auto-generated name like "temp-<timestamp>".
    70→
    71→Environment variables can set defaults for commonly used flags:
    72→  SANDCASTLE_HOME=1    equivalent to --home
    73→  SANDCASTLE_DATA=.    equivalent to --data (value is the subpath, "." or "1" for root)
    74→  SANDCASTLE_RM=1      equivalent to --rm
    75→
    76→Flags explicitly passed on the command line take precedence over environment variables.`,
    77→	Args: cobra.MaximumNArgs(1),
    78→	PreRun: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) {
    79→		// Priority: explicit flag > env var > config preference

   155→		// Print active options (use local flags — they reflect what was actually requested)

   225→		var sshErr error

   232→		if sandboxRemove {

   241→				} else {

   243→				}
   244→			}
   245→		}

   247→		return sshErr
   248→	},

   403→
   404→		sandbox, err := findSandboxByName(client, args[0])
   405→		if err != nil {
   406→			return err
   407→		}
   408→
   409→		sandbox, err = client.StartSandbox(sandbox.ID)
   410→		if err != nil {
   411→			return err
   412→		}
   413→
   414→		fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q started.\n", sandbox.Name)
   415→		return nil
   416→	},
   417→}
   418→
   419→var stopCmd = &cobra.Command{
   420→	Use:     "stop <name>",
   421→	Aliases: []string{"dn"},
   422→	Short:   "Stop a running sandbox",
   423→	Args:  cobra.ExactArgs(1),
   424→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   425→		client, err := api.NewClient()
   426→		if err != nil {
   427→			return err
   428→		}
   429→		printServer(client)
   430→
   431→		sandbox, err := findSandboxByName(client, args[0])
   432→		if err != nil {
   433→			return err
   434→		}
   435→
   436→		sandbox, err = client.StopSandbox(sandbox.ID)
   437→		if err != nil {
   438→			return err
   439→		}
   440→
   441→		fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q stopped.\n", sandbox.Name)
   442→		return nil
   443→	},
   444→}
   445→
   446→var useCmd = &cobra.Command{
   447→	Use:   "use [name]",
   448→	Short: "Show or set active server/sandbox",
   449→	Long: `Without arguments, shows the current server and active sandbox.
   450→With an argument, switches the active server or sandbox.
   451→
   452→Examples:
   453→  sandcastle use                  # Show current server and sandbox
   454→  sandcastle use my-sandbox       # Set active sandbox
   455→  sandcastle use prod             # Switch to server "prod" (if configured)`,
   456→	Args: cobra.MaximumNArgs(1),
   457→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   458→		cfg, err := config.Load()
   459→		if err != nil {
   460→			return err
   461→		}
   462→
   463→		// No args: list all servers, highlight active
   464→		if len(args) == 0 {
   465→			if len(cfg.Servers) == 0 {
   466→				fmt.Println("No servers configured — run: sandcastle login <url>")
   467→				return nil
   468→			}
   469→
   470→			aliases := make([]string, 0, len(cfg.Servers))
   471→			for alias := range cfg.Servers {
   472→				aliases = append(aliases, alias)
   473→			}
   474→			sort.Strings(aliases)
   475→
   476→			for _, alias := range aliases {
   477→				srv := cfg.Servers[alias]
   478→				if alias == cfg.CurrentServer {
   479→					fmt.Printf("  \033[1m* %-16s\033[0m %s\n", alias, srv.URL)
   480→				} else {
   481→					fmt.Printf("    %-16s %s\n", alias, srv.URL)
   482→				}
   483→			}
   484→
   485→			return nil
   486→		}
   487→
   488→		name := args[0]
   489→
   490→		// Check if name matches a server alias or URL
   491→		if _, ok := cfg.Servers[name]; ok {
   492→			cfg.CurrentServer = name
   493→			if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
   494→				return err
   495→			}
   496→			fmt.Printf("Switched to server %s (%s)\n", name, cfg.Servers[name].URL)
   497→			return nil
   498→		}
   499→
   500→		// Try matching by URL
   501→		for alias, srv := range cfg.Servers {
   502→			if srv.URL == strings.TrimRight(name, "/") {
   503→				cfg.CurrentServer = alias
   504→				if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
   505→					return err
   506→				}
   507→				fmt.Printf("Switched to server %s (%s)\n", alias, srv.URL)
   508→				return nil
   509→			}
   510→		}
   511→
   512→		return fmt.Errorf("server %q not found — run: sandcastle use", name)
   513→	},
   514→}
   515→
   516→var setCmd = &cobra.Command{
   517→	Use:   "set <name> <temp|keep>",
   518→	Short: "Toggle sandbox between temporary and kept",
   519→	Long: `Toggle a sandbox between temporary (auto-remove on exit) and kept.
   520→
   521→  temp   Mark as temporary — will be removed when the CLI session exits
   522→  keep   Mark as kept — will not be auto-removed`,
   523→	Args: cobra.ExactArgs(2),
   524→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   525→		client, err := api.NewClient()
   526→		if err != nil {
   527→			return err
   528→		}
   529→		printServer(client)
   530→
   531→		sandbox, err := findSandboxByName(client, args[0])
   532→		if err != nil {
   533→			return err
   534→		}
   535→
   536→		mode := strings.ToLower(args[1])
   537→		var temp bool
   538→		switch mode {
   539→		case "temp":
   540→			temp = true
   541→		case "keep":
   542→			temp = false
   543→		default:
   544→			return fmt.Errorf("unknown mode %q: use \"temp\" or \"keep\"", args[1])
   545→		}
   546→
   547→		sandbox, err = client.UpdateSandbox(sandbox.ID, api.UpdateSandboxRequest{Temporary: &temp})
   548→		if err != nil {
   549→			return err
   550→		}
   551→
   552→		if temp {
   553→			fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q set to temporary (will be removed on exit).\n", sandbox.Name)
   554→		} else {
   555→			fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q set to keep (will not be removed on exit).\n", sandbox.Name)
   556→		}
   557→		return nil
   558→	},
   559→}
   560→
   561→var renameCmd = &cobra.Command{
   562→	Use:     "rename <name> <new-name>",
   563→	Aliases: []string{"mv"},
   564→	Short:   "Rename a sandbox",
   565→	Args:    cobra.ExactArgs(2),
   566→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   567→		client, err := api.NewClient()
   568→		if err != nil {
   569→			return err
   570→		}
   571→		printServer(client)
   572→
   573→		sandbox, err := findSandboxByName(client, args[0])
   574→		if err != nil {
   575→			return err
   576→		}
   577→
   578→		newName := args[1]
   579→		sandbox, err = client.UpdateSandbox(sandbox.ID, api.UpdateSandboxRequest{Name: &newName})
   580→		if err != nil {
   581→			return err
   582→		}
   583→
   584→		fmt.Printf("Sandbox renamed to %q.\n", sandbox.Name)
   585→		return nil
   586→	},
   587→}
   588→
   589→func envTruthy(key string) bool {
   590→	v := strings.ToLower(os.Getenv(key))
   591→	return v == "1" || v == "true" || v == "yes"
   592→}
   593→
   594→func findSandboxByName(client *api.Client, name string) (*api.Sandbox, error) {
   595→	sandboxes, err := client.ListSandboxes()
   596→	if err != nil {
   597→		return nil, err
   598→	}
   599→	for _, s := range sandboxes {
   600→		if s.Name == name {
   601→			return &s, nil
   602→		}
   603→	}
   604→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("sandbox %q not found", name)
   605→}
   606→

     1→<% content_for(:title) { "Create Sandcastle" } %>
     2→
     3→<div class="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 py-8">
     4→  <div class="mb-6">
     5→    <h1 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">Create Sandcastle</h1>
     6→    <p class="text-gray-500 mt-1">Configure and create a new sandbox environment</p>
     7→  </div>
     8→
     9→  <%= form_with url: sandboxes_path, method: :post, class: "space-y-6" do |form| %>
    10→    <%# Basic Information %>
    11→    <div class="bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-6 space-y-4">
    12→      <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Basic Information</h2>
    13→
    14→      <div>
    15→        <%= form.label :name, "Sandbox Name", class: "block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1" %>
    16→        <%= form.text_field :name,
    17→              required: true,
    18→              pattern: "[a-z][a-z0-9_\\-]*",
    19→              maxlength: 63,
    20→              placeholder: "",
    21→              id: "sandbox_name",
    22→              class: "w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500" %>
    23→        <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">Lowercase letters, numbers, hyphens, and underscores only</p>
    24→      </div>
    25→
    26→      <div>
    27→        <%= form.label :image, "Container Image", class: "block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1" %>
    28→        <%= form.text_field :image,
    29→              placeholder: "ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest",
    30→              class: "w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500" %>
    31→        <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">Leave empty to use the default image</p>
    32→      </div>
    33→    </div>
    34→
    35→    <%# Restore from Snapshot %>
    36→    <% if @snapshots.any? %>
    37→      <div class="bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-6 space-y-4">
    38→        <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Restore from Snapshot</h2>
    39→
    40→        <div>
    41→          <%= form.label :snapshot, "Snapshot", class: "block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1" %>
    42→          <%= form.select :snapshot,
    43→                options_for_select(
    44→                  [["None (fresh sandbox)", ""]] +
    45→                  @snapshots.map { |s|
    46→                    label = s[:name]
    47→                    label += " — #{s[:label]}" if s[:label].present?
    48→                    label += " [#{s[:layers].join(', ')}]" if s[:layers].present?
    49→                    label += " (from #{s[:source_sandbox]})" if s[:source_sandbox].present?
    50→                    [label, s[:name]]
    51→                  },
    52→                  params[:snapshot]
    53→                ),
    54→                {},
    55→                class: "w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500" %>
    56→          <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">
    57→            Create a new sandbox from an existing snapshot.
    58→            <% unless @btrfs_available %>
    59→              (Only container layer available — BTRFS not detected)
    60→            <% end %>
    61→          </p>
    62→        </div>
    63→      </div>
    64→    <% end %>
    65→
    66→    <%# Storage Options %>
    67→    <div class="bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-6 space-y-4">
    68→      <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Storage Options</h2>
    69→
    70→      <div class="space-y-3">
    71→        <label class="flex items-start gap-3 cursor-pointer">
    72→          <%= form.check_box :persistent, class: "mt-1 h-4 w-4 text-blue-600 border-gray-300 rounded focus:ring-blue-500" %>
    73→          <div class="flex-1">
    74→            <div class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">Enable persistent volume</div>
    75→            <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Create a persistent /data volume that survives sandbox recreation</p>
    76→          </div>
    77→        </label>
    78→
    79→        <label class="flex items-start gap-3 cursor-pointer">

    81→          <div class="flex-1">

    93→        </div>

    96→
    97→    <%# Network Options %>
    98→    <% if Current.user.tailscale_enabled? %>
    99→      <div class="bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-6 space-y-4">
   100→        <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Network Options</h2>
   101→
   102→        <label class="flex items-start gap-3 cursor-pointer">
   103→          <%= form.check_box :tailscale, checked: true, class: "mt-1 h-4 w-4 text-purple-600 border-gray-300 rounded focus:ring-purple-500" %>
   104→          <div class="flex-1">
   105→            <div class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">Connect to Tailscale network</div>
   106→            <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Automatically connect this sandbox to your Tailscale network</p>
   107→          </div>
   108→        </label>
   109→      </div>
   110→    <% end %>
   111→
   112→    <%# VNC / Display Options %>
   113→    <div class="bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-6 space-y-4">
   114→      <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">VNC / Display</h2>
   115→
   116→      <label class="flex items-start gap-3 cursor-pointer">
   117→        <%= form.check_box :vnc_enabled, checked: true,
   118→              id: "sandbox_vnc_enabled",
   119→              class: "mt-1 h-4 w-4 text-blue-600 border-gray-300 rounded focus:ring-blue-500" %>
   120→        <div class="flex-1">
   121→          <div class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">Enable VNC display</div>
   122→          <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Start a virtual display (Xvnc) for GUI apps and browser access</p>
   123→        </div>
   124→      </label>
   125→
   126→      <div id="vnc_options_row" class="space-y-4">
   127→        <div>
   128→          <%= form.label :vnc_geometry, "Screen Resolution", class: "block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1" %>
   129→          <%= form.select :vnc_geometry,
   130→                options_for_select([
   131→                  ["1280×900 (default)", "1280x900"],
   132→                  ["1366×768", "1366x768"],
   133→                  ["1440×900", "1440x900"],
   134→                  ["1600×900", "1600x900"],
   135→                  ["1920×1080 (Full HD)", "1920x1080"],
   136→                  ["2560×1440 (2K)", "2560x1440"]
   137→                ], "1280x900"),
   138→                {},
   139→                class: "w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500" %>
   140→        </div>
   141→
   142→        <div>
   143→          <%= form.label :vnc_depth, "Color Depth", class: "block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1" %>
   144→          <%= form.select :vnc_depth,
   145→                options_for_select([
   146→                  ["24-bit (default)", 24],
   147→                  ["32-bit", 32],
   148→                  ["16-bit", 16],
   149→                  ["8-bit", 8]
   150→                ], 24),
   151→                {},
   152→                class: "w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500" %>
   153→        </div>
   154→      </div>
   155→    </div>
   156→
   157→    <%# Actions %>
   158→    <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-4">
   159→      <%= link_to "Cancel", root_path, class: "text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900" %>
   160→      <%= form.submit "Create Sandcastle", class: "px-6 py-2.5 bg-blue-600 text-white font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-blue-200" %>
   161→    </div>
   162→  <% end %>
   163→</div>
   164→
   165→<script>
   166→(function() {
   167→  var adjectives = ["swift", "brave", "wild", "calm", "bold", "wise", "cool", "warm", "bright", "dark", "silent", "loud", "quick", "slow", "happy", "zen", "cosmic", "electric", "quantum", "digital"];
   168→  var nouns = ["fox", "wolf", "eagle", "tiger", "bear", "shark", "hawk", "lion", "dragon", "phoenix", "cobra", "falcon", "raven", "panther", "viper", "lynx", "orca", "jaguar", "puma", "leopard"];
   169→
   170→  function generateName() {
   171→    var adj = adjectives[Math.floor(Math.random() * adjectives.length)];
   172→    var noun = nouns[Math.floor(Math.random() * nouns.length)];
   173→    return adj + "-" + noun;
   174→  }
   175→
   176→  var nameField = document.getElementById("sandbox_name");
   177→  if (nameField) {
   178→    nameField.value = generateName();
   179→  }
   180→
   181→  var vncCheckbox = document.getElementById("sandbox_vnc_enabled");
   182→  var vncOptionsRow = document.getElementById("vnc_options_row");
   183→  if (vncCheckbox && vncOptionsRow) {
   184→    function toggleVncOptions() {
   185→      vncOptionsRow.style.display = vncCheckbox.checked ? "" : "none";
   186→    }
   187→    vncCheckbox.addEventListener("change", toggleVncOptions);
   188→  }
   189→})();
   190→</script>
   191→

}

}

**Migration** — Adds `docker_enabled` (bool, default true) to sandboxes + 4 default columns to settings: `default_vnc_enabled`, `default_mount_home`, `default_data_path`, `default_docker_enabled`

**Setting model** — Validations for new boolean fields

**SandboxManager** — Passes `SANDCASTLE_DOCKER_ENABLED` env var to container

**Entrypoint** — Skips `dockerd` startup when `SANDCASTLE_DOCKER_ENABLED=0`

**Admin Settings UI** — New "Sandbox Defaults" section with VNC, mount home, Docker daemon, and data path controls

**Sandbox creation form** — Uses admin defaults for checkboxes/fields; new Docker daemon toggle under renamed "Services" section

**API controller** — Falls back to `Setting.instance` defaults when params not explicitly provided; includes `docker_enabled` in JSON response

**Web controller** — Accepts `docker_enabled` param; passes `@defaults` to form

**CLI** — New `--no-docker` flag; `DockerEnabled` in request/response types

**Guide page** — Documents `--no-docker` and `--no-vnc` combo

**Dockerfile.base** — Removes DinD status message from bash.bashrc

Want me to commit and push?

The `ENV MISE_DATA_DIR` and `MISE_CONFIG_DIR` are set, but during Docker build the `RUN` commands run in a new shell that inherits `ENV` — so that should work. Let me check if there's an issue with mise ignoring ENV during build. The real problem might be that `mise use --global` still writes to `~/.config/mise` instead of `MISE_CONFIG_DIR`. Let me also split claude install to use the new standalone binary approach and ensure codex still gets npm-installed via mise.

Pushed. Two key fixes:

1. **Claude Code** — now installed as a standalone native binary from GCS instead of via npm (no Node.js dependency)
2. **mise config dir** — explicitly `mkdir -p /usr/local/etc/mise` before `mise use --global` so the global config file lands in the shared dir, not `~/.config/mise`

The base image rebuild you triggered earlier will pick up the previous commit. You may want to trigger another rebuild for this one, or wait for the next one.

> AGENT

Looking at the process list, here's the memory breakdown:

| Process | RSS | Toggleable? |
|---------|-----|-------------|
| `dockerd` + `containerd` | **~125 MB** | `docker_enabled` (just added) |
| `Xvnc` + `openbox` + `websockify` | **~45 MB** | `vnc_enabled` (existing) |
| `ttyd` x2 | ~2 MB | not yet |
| `sshd` | ~8 MB | essential |

**The big win is already there.** A sandbox with `--no-docker --no-vnc` drops **~170 MB** per container. With `dockerd` being the single heaviest process, the `docker_enabled` toggle we just shipped is the biggest lever.

The two `ttyd` instances are negligible (1 MB each), but they do run even when nobody uses the web terminal. Worth making optional? Or is the focus on setting good defaults (e.g., defaulting docker off for lightweight sandboxes)?

Now I have everything I need. Let me implement this efficiently. The approach:

1. **API routes**: `POST /api/sandboxes/:id/services/:service/start|stop` with `?save=true`
2. **SandboxManager**: `exec_service_start`/`exec_service_stop` methods that run commands inside the container
3. **API controller**: New `service_start`/`service_stop` actions
4. **CLI**: `sandcastle docker start|stop [sandbox] [--save]` and `sandcastle vnc start|stop [sandbox] [--save]`

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/sandbox_manager.rb",
  "old_string": "  def logs(sandbox:, tail: 200, timestamps: false)",
  "new_string": "  SERVICES = %w[docker vnc].freeze\n\n  def service_start(sandbox:, service:)\n    raise Error, \"Unknown service: #{service}\" unless SERVICES.include?(service)\n    raise Error, \"Sandbox has no container\" if sandbox.container_id.blank?\n\n    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)\n    user = sandbox.user.name\n\n    case service\n    when \"docker\"\n      container.exec([ \"bash\", \"-c\", \"pgrep -x dockerd > /dev/null && echo already_running && exit 0; dockerd --storage-driver=overlay2 --mtu=$(ip link show eth0 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'mtu \\\\K[0-9]+' || echo 1500) &>/var/log/dockerd.log & echo started\" ])\n    when \"vnc\"\n      geometry = sandbox.vnc_geometry || \"1280x900\"\n      depth = sandbox.vnc_depth || 24\n      container.exec([ \"bash\", \"-c\", \"pgrep -x Xvnc > /dev/null && echo already_running && exit 0; su -s /bin/bash #{user} -c 'Xvnc :99 -rfbport 5900 -SecurityTypes None -AlwaysShared -geometry #{geometry} -depth #{depth} &>/var/log/xvnc.log &' && su -s /bin/bash #{user} -c 'DISPLAY=:99 openbox &>/var/log/openbox.log &' && websockify -addr :6080 -target localhost:5900 -url /websockify &>/var/log/websockify.log & echo started\" ])\n    end\n  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError\n    raise Error, \"Container not found\"\n  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e\n    raise Error, \"Failed to start #{service}: #{e.message}\"\n  end\n\n  def service_stop(sandbox:, service:)\n    raise Error, \"Unknown service: #{service}\" unless SERVICES.include?(service)\n    raise Error, \"Sandbox has no container\" if sandbox.container_id.blank?\n\n    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)\n\n    case service\n    when \"docker\"\n      container.exec([ \"bash\", \"-c\", \"pkill -x dockerd 2>/dev/null; pkill -x containerd 2>/dev/null; echo stopped\" ])\n    when \"vnc\"\n      container.exec([ \"bash\", \"-c\", \"pkill -x websockify 2>/dev/null; pkill -x openbox 2>/dev/null; pkill -x Xvnc 2>/dev/null; echo stopped\" ])\n    end\n  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError\n    raise Error, \"Container not found\"\n  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e\n    raise Error, \"Failed to stop #{service}: #{e.message}\"\n  end\n\n  def logs(sandbox:, tail: 200, timestamps: false)"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/api/sandboxes_controller.rb",
  "old_string": "    def tailscale_connect",
  "new_string": "    def service_start\n      service = params[:service]\n      SandboxManager.new.service_start(sandbox: @sandbox, service: service)\n      if params[:save].present?\n        case service\n        when \"docker\" then @sandbox.update!(docker_enabled: true)\n        when \"vnc\" then @sandbox.update!(vnc_enabled: true)\n        end\n      end\n      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox.reload)\n    end\n\n    def service_stop\n      service = params[:service]\n      SandboxManager.new.service_stop(sandbox: @sandbox, service: service)\n      if params[:save].present?\n        case service\n        when \"docker\" then @sandbox.update!(docker_enabled: false)\n        when \"vnc\" then @sandbox.update!(vnc_enabled: false)\n        end\n      end\n      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox.reload)\n    end\n\n    def tailscale_connect"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/vendor/sandcastle-cli/cmd/services.go",
  "content": "package cmd\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\n\t\"github.com/sandcastle/cli/api\"\n\t\"github.com/spf13/cobra\"\n)\n\nvar serviceSave bool\n\nfunc init() {\n\trootCmd.AddCommand(dockerCmd)\n\trootCmd.AddCommand(vncCmd)\n\n\tfor _, cmd := range []*cobra.Command{dockerStartCmd, dockerStopCmd, vncStartCmd, vncStopCmd} {\n\t\tcmd.Flags().BoolVar(&serviceSave, \"save\", false, \"Persist this setting so the service stays on/off after container restart\")\n\t}\n\n\tdockerCmd.AddCommand(dockerStartCmd)\n\tdockerCmd.AddCommand(dockerStopCmd)\n\tvncCmd.AddCommand(vncStartCmd)\n\tvncCmd.AddCommand(vncStopCmd)\n}\n\nvar dockerCmd = &cobra.Command{\n\tUse:   \"docker\",\n\tShort: \"Manage Docker daemon inside a sandbox\",\n}\n\nvar vncCmd = &cobra.Command{\n\tUse:   \"vnc\",\n\tShort: \"Manage VNC display server inside a sandbox\",\n}\n\nvar dockerStartCmd = &cobra.Command{\n\tUse:   \"start <sandbox>\",\n\tShort: \"Start Docker daemon inside a sandbox\",\n\tArgs:  cobra.ExactArgs(1),\n\tRunE:  serviceRunE(\"docker\", \"start\"),\n}\n\nvar dockerStopCmd = &cobra.Command{\n\tUse:   \"stop <sandbox>\",\n\tShort: \"Stop Docker daemon inside a sandbox\",\n\tArgs:  cobra.ExactArgs(1),\n\tRunE:  serviceRunE(\"docker\", \"stop\"),\n}\n\nvar vncStartCmd = &cobra.Command{\n\tUse:   \"start <sandbox>\",\n\tShort: \"Start VNC display server inside a sandbox\",\n\tArgs:  cobra.ExactArgs(1),\n\tRunE:  serviceRunE(\"vnc\", \"start\"),\n}\n\nvar vncStopCmd = &cobra.Command{\n\tUse:   \"stop <sandbox>\",\n\tShort: \"Stop VNC display server inside a sandbox\",\n\tArgs:  cobra.ExactArgs(1),\n\tRunE:  serviceRunE(\"vnc\", \"stop\"),\n}\n\nfunc serviceRunE(service, action string) func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {\n\treturn func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {\n\t\tclient, err := api.NewClient()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tprintServer(client)\n\n\t\tsandbox, err := findSandboxByName(client, args[0])\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar result *api.Sandbox\n\t\tswitch action {\n\t\tcase \"start\":\n\t\t\tresult, err = client.ServiceStart(sandbox.ID, service, serviceSave)\n\t\tcase \"stop\":\n\t\t\tresult, err = client.ServiceStop(sandbox.ID, service, serviceSave)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tlabel := service\n\t\tif service == \"docker\" {\n\t\t\tlabel = \"Docker daemon\"\n\t\t} else if service == \"vnc\" {\n\t\t\tlabel = \"VNC display\"\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tverb := \"started\"\n\t\tif action == \"stop\" {\n\t\t\tverb = \"stopped\"\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"%s %s in sandbox %q.\", label, verb, result.Name)\n\t\tif serviceSave {\n\t\t\tfmt.Print(\" (saved for restart)\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tfmt.Println()\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Released **v0.8.49**. New commands:

```bash
# Stop Docker daemon to save ~125MB RAM
sandcastle docker stop my-dev
sandcastle docker stop my-dev --save   # stays off after restart

# Stop VNC to save ~45MB RAM
sandcastle vnc stop my-dev
sandcastle vnc stop my-dev --save

# Start them back up
sandcastle docker start my-dev
sandcastle vnc start my-dev --save     # stays on after restart
```

> AGENT

Fixed. `resource :status` was looking for `Api::StatusesController` (Rails pluralizes singular resources), but the file is `StatusController`. Added explicit `controller: "status"` to the route. You can hotpatch this to production:

```bash
scp config/routes.rb sandcastle@sandman:/tmp/routes.rb
ssh sandman 'docker cp /tmp/routes.rb sandcastle-web:/rails/config/routes.rb && docker restart sandcastle-web'
```

> AGENT

The issue is Turbo Drive page caching. When the modal is open and Turbo caches the page, it caches it with the `hidden` class removed. When you navigate back, the cached version shows the modal open.

The fix is to add a `data-turbo-cache="false"` on the modal, or better yet, close all modals before Turbo caches the page.

> TOOL

Fixed. The issue was Turbo Drive caching the page while the snapshot modal was open — when you navigate back, the cached HTML still has the modal visible (no `hidden` class). Now `turbo:before-cache` re-hides all snapshot modals before caching.

     1→# Thin layer on top of the pre-built base image.
     2→# Changes here (entrypoint, config, scripts) build in seconds.
     3→# To rebuild the base: trigger the "Rebuild Sandbox Base Image" workflow
     4→# or change files in images/sandbox/base/.
     5→FROM ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox-base:latest
     6→
     7→ARG SANDCASTLE_VERSION=dev
     8→
     9→# Bake version into the image
    10→RUN echo "${SANDCASTLE_VERSION}" > /etc/sandcastle-version
    11→
    12→COPY entrypoint.sh /entrypoint.sh
    13→COPY docker-restart.sh /usr/local/bin/docker-restart
    14→RUN chmod +x /entrypoint.sh /usr/local/bin/docker-restart
    15→
    16→COPY startchrome.sh /usr/local/bin/google-chrome
    17→RUN chmod +x /usr/local/bin/google-chrome \
    18→    && ln -sf /usr/local/bin/google-chrome /usr/local/bin/startchrome
    19→
    20→COPY tmux.conf /etc/tmux.conf
    21→COPY gitconfig /etc/gitconfig
    22→
    23→LABEL org.opencontainers.image.source="https://github.com/thieso2/sandcastle"
    24→LABEL org.opencontainers.image.description="Sandcastle sandbox image — Ubuntu 25.10 with Docker-in-Docker, SSH, and dev tools"
    25→LABEL org.opencontainers.image.licenses="MIT"
    26→
    27→WORKDIR /workspace
    28→EXPOSE 22 5900 6080 7681 7682
    29→EXPOSE 60000-61000/udp
    30→
    31→ENTRYPOINT ["/entrypoint.sh"]
    32→
```

```
     1→module Admin
     2→  class DockerController < BaseController
     3→    INFRA_CONTAINERS = %w[
     4→      sandcastle-traefik
     5→      sandcastle-postgres-1
     6→      sandcastle-web
     7→      sandcastle-worker
     8→    ].freeze
     9→
    10→    def index
    11→      authorize :user, :index?
    12→      @containers = fetch_containers
    13→    end
    14→
    15→    def logs
    16→      authorize :user, :index?
    17→      name = params[:id]
    18→      unless INFRA_CONTAINERS.include?(name)
    19→        return render json: { error: "Not an infra container" }, status: :forbidden
    20→      end
    21→
    22→      tail = (params[:tail] || 200).to_i.clamp(1, 2000)
    23→      container = Docker::Container.get(name)
    24→      raw = container.logs(stdout: true, stderr: true, tail: tail, timestamps: true)
    25→      lines = strip_docker_stream_headers(raw)
    26→      render json: { container: name, lines: lines }
    27→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
    28→      render json: { error: "Container not found: #{name}" }, status: :not_found
    29→    rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    30→      render json: { error: e.message }, status: :unprocessable_entity
    31→    end
    32→
    33→    private
    34→
    35→    def fetch_containers
    36→      all = Docker::Container.all(all: true)
    37→      INFRA_CONTAINERS.map do |name|
    38→        c = all.find { |ct| ct.info.dig("Names")&.include?("/#{name}") }
    39→        if c
    40→          state = c.info["State"]
    41→          status = c.info["Status"]
    42→          created = Time.at(c.info["Created"]).utc
    43→          { name: name, state: state, status: status, created: created, exists: true }
    44→        else
    45→          { name: name, exists: false }
    46→        end
    47→      end
    48→    rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    49→      INFRA_CONTAINERS.map { |name| { name: name, exists: false, error: e.message } }
    50→    end
    51→
    52→    # Docker log streams have 8-byte headers per frame; strip them.
    53→    def strip_docker_stream_headers(raw)
    54→      raw.encode("UTF-8", invalid: :replace, undef: :replace)
    55→         .lines
    56→         .map { |line| line.sub(/\A[\x00-\x02].{7}/, "").rstrip }
    57→         .reject(&:empty?)
    58→    end
    59→  end
    60→end
    61→
```

    97→  function toggleLogs(button) {
    98→    var name = button.dataset.container;
    99→    var panel = document.getElementById("logs-" + name);
   100→    if (panel.classList.contains("hidden")) {
   101→      panel.classList.remove("hidden");
   102→      button.textContent = "Hide Logs";
   103→      loadLogs(name, 200);
   104→    } else {
   105→      panel.classList.add("hidden");
   106→      button.textContent = "Show Logs";
   107→    }
   108→  }
   109→
   110→  function reloadLogs(name, tail) {
   111→    if (!tail) {
   112→      var select = document.querySelector("#logs-" + name + " select");
   113→      tail = select ? select.value : 200;
   114→    }
   115→    loadLogs(name, tail);
   116→  }
   117→
   118→  function loadLogs(name, tail) {
   119→    var panel = document.getElementById("logs-" + name);
   120→    var output = panel.querySelector(".log-output");
   121→    var countSpan = panel.querySelector(".line-count");
   122→    output.textContent = "Loading...";
   123→    countSpan.textContent = tail;
   124→
   125→    fetch("/admin/docker/" + encodeURIComponent(name) + "/logs?tail=" + tail, {
   126→      headers: { "Accept": "application/json" }
   127→    })
   128→      .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
   129→      .then(function(data) {
   130→        if (data.error) {
   131→          output.textContent = "Error: " + data.error;
   132→        } else {
   133→          output.textContent = data.lines.join("\n");
   134→          output.scrollTop = output.scrollHeight;
   135→        }
   136→      })
   137→      .catch(function(e) {
   138→        output.textContent = "Failed to fetch logs: " + e.message;
   139→      });
   140→  }
   141→</script>

loadLogs(currentContainer);\n    }, 3000);\n  }\n\n  function stopAutoTail() {\n    if (tailInterval) {\n      clearInterval(tailInterval);\n      tailInterval = null;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Load first tab on page load and start auto-tail\n  if (currentContainer) {\n    loadLogs(currentContainer);\n    startAutoTail();\n  }\n\n  // Stop polling when leaving the page\n  document.addEventListener(\"turbo:before-cache\", stopAutoTail);\n  document.addEventListener(\"turbo:before-visit\", stopAutoTail);\n</script>\n"
}

> AGENT

The `card` action is called by Turbo to refresh a sandbox card, but after the sandbox is destroyed/archived it's excluded by `policy_scope` (which filters out destroyed/archived). The fix is to handle the 404 gracefully.

    87→      ip = container.info.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks", network, "IPAddress")

    89→    end

    92→  end
    93→end

> AGENT

The `card` action doesn't handle `RecordNotFound` — when a sandbox gets destroyed while the dashboard is open, the Turbo polling still tries to refresh the card. Simple fix: return a Turbo Stream that removes the card.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_014or3PwuWYpLRGhowbtotaM
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/dashboard_controller.rb",
  "old_string": "  def card\n    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).find(params[:id])\n    authorize sandbox\n    vnc_active = VncManager.new.active?(sandbox: sandbox)\n    tailscale_ip = sandbox.tailscale? ? TailscaleManager.new.sandbox_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox) : nil\n    render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(helpers.dom_id(sandbox), partial: \"dashboard/sandbox\", locals: { sandbox: sandbox, vnc_active: vnc_active, tailscale_ip: tailscale_ip })\n  end",
  "new_string": "  def card\n    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).find(params[:id])\n    authorize sandbox\n    vnc_active = VncManager.new.active?(sandbox: sandbox)\n    tailscale_ip = sandbox.tailscale? ? TailscaleManager.new.sandbox_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox) : nil\n    render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(helpers.dom_id(sandbox), partial: \"dashboard/sandbox\", locals: { sandbox: sandbox, vnc_active: vnc_active, tailscale_ip: tailscale_ip })\n  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound\n    # Sandbox was destroyed/archived while the dashboard was open — remove the card\n    render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.remove(\"sandbox_#{params[:id]}\")\n  end"
}
```

     1→class SandboxProvisionJob < ApplicationJob
     2→  queue_as :default
     3→
     4→  def perform(sandbox_id:)
     5→    sandbox = Sandbox.find(sandbox_id)
     6→    return if sandbox.status == "running" # Idempotent
     7→
     8→    sandbox.start_job("creating")
     9→    manager = SandboxManager.new
    10→
    11→    begin
    12→      # Directory creation
    13→      manager.ensure_mount_dirs(sandbox.user, sandbox)
    14→
    15→      # Image pull (can be slow)
    16→      manager.ensure_image(sandbox.image)
    17→
    18→      # Container creation and start
    19→      manager.create_container_and_start(sandbox: sandbox, user: sandbox.user)
    20→
    21→      # Connect to Tailscale if enabled
    22→      if sandbox.tailscale? && sandbox.user.tailscale_enabled?
    23→        TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox)
    24→      end
    25→
    26→      sandbox.update!(status: "running")
    27→      sandbox.finish_job
    28→
    29→    rescue => e
    30→      Rails.logger.error("SandboxProvisionJob failed: #{e.message}\n#{e.backtrace.join("\n")}")
    31→      sandbox.fail_job("Failed to create: #{e.message}")
    32→      sandbox.update!(status: "destroyed")
    33→
    34→      raise # Re-raise for Solid Queue retry
    35→    end
    36→  end
    37→end
    38→

> AGENT

The issue is clear — jobs only log on errors. There's no logging when a job starts, completes, or what it's doing. The simplest approach: add an `around_perform` callback in `ApplicationJob` that logs start/finish/error for all jobs.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/jobs/application_job.rb",
  "content": "class ApplicationJob < ActiveJob::Base\n  # Automatically retry jobs that encountered a deadlock\n  # retry_on ActiveRecord::Deadlocked\n\n  # Most jobs are safe to ignore if the underlying records are no longer available\n  # discard_on ActiveJob::DeserializationError\n\n  around_perform do |job, block|\n    args_summary = job.arguments.map { |a| a.is_a?(Hash) ? a.map { |k, v| \"#{k}=#{v}\" }.join(\" \") : a.to_s }.join(\", \")\n    logger.info { \"[Job] #{job.class.name} started (#{args_summary})\" }\n    started = Process.clock_gettime(Process::CLOCK_MONOTONIC)\n    block.call\n    elapsed = (Process.clock_gettime(Process::CLOCK_MONOTONIC) - started).round(1)\n    logger.info { \"[Job] #{job.class.name} completed in #{elapsed}s\" }\n  rescue => e\n    elapsed = (Process.clock_gettime(Process::CLOCK_MONOTONIC) - started).round(1)\n    logger.error { \"[Job] #{job.class.name} FAILED after #{elapsed}s: #{e.class}: #{e.message}\" }\n    raise\n  end\nend\n"
}
```

> AGENT

Empty output — SSH can't reach the server. But I can see the problem from the code.

The issue: `config.logger = ActiveSupport::TaggedLogging.logger(STDOUT)` only works for the web process. Solid Queue runs `bin/jobs` which boots Rails, but Solid Queue has its own logger. By default it may log to a file or use a separate logger that doesn't go to STDOUT.

Let me check Solid Queue config.

> TOOL

In development, there's no `config.logger` set — Rails defaults to logging to `log/development.log`, not STDOUT. So the worker's logs go to a file inside the container, not to `docker logs`.

The fix: configure the logger to STDOUT when running in a container (both dev and prod). Let me also set Solid Queue's logger explicitly.

> AGENT

Two problems:
1. Development doesn't log to STDOUT (defaults to `log/development.log`)
2. Solid Queue's own supervisor logging may not use the Rails logger

Fix both: log to STDOUT in development (since it runs in Docker), and explicitly set Solid Queue's logger.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01JQhhTK32aYfHUnMWpbCqrK
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/config/environments/development.rb",
  "old_string": "  # Use Solid Queue for background jobs\n  config.active_job.queue_adapter = :solid_queue\n  config.solid_queue.connects_to = { database: { writing: :queue } }",
  "new_string": "  # Log to STDOUT when running in Docker (so `docker logs` works)\n  if ENV[\"RAILS_LOG_TO_STDOUT\"].present? || File.exist?(\"/.dockerenv\")\n    config.logger = ActiveSupport::TaggedLogging.logger(STDOUT)\n    config.log_level = ENV.fetch(\"RAILS_LOG_LEVEL\", \"debug\")\n  end\n\n  # Use Solid Queue for background jobs\n  config.active_job.queue_adapter = :solid_queue\n  config.solid_queue.connects_to = { database: { writing: :queue } }\n  config.solid_queue.silence_polling = true"
}
```

> AGENT

Fixed. Two issues:

1. **Dev logger went to file, not STDOUT** — In development, Rails defaults to `log/development.log`. Now detects Docker (via `/.dockerenv`) and switches to STDOUT so `docker logs -f sandcastle-worker` shows output.

2. **Solid Queue polling noise** — Added `silence_polling = true` in both envs to suppress the constant "polling for jobs" messages, so your actual job logs (`[Job] SandboxProvisionJob started...`) are visible.

Restart the worker to pick it up: `docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml restart worker`

> AGENT

There it is. Development uses `adapter: async` which only works **within the same process**. The worker is a separate container/process, so its Turbo broadcasts never reach the web process's Action Cable.

Fix: use `solid_cable` in development too (just like production), since the dev setup already has the cable database configured.

> TOOL

> AGENT

The problem: `cable.yml` had `adapter: async` for development, which only works within a single process. The worker runs in a separate container, so when it updates a sandbox to "running" and triggers `after_update_commit :broadcast_replace_to_dashboard`, the broadcast never reaches the web process's Action Cable.

Fixed by switching to `solid_cable` (same as production) — both web and worker share the cable database, so broadcasts propagate. Restart both containers to pick it up.

   192→             data-sparkline-stats-url-value="<%= stats_sandbox_path(sandbox, format: :json) %>"

   217→      <button onclick="document.getElementById('snap-modal-<%= sandbox.id %>').classList.add('hidden')" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 text-lg leading-none">✕</button>

   220→      <%= f.text_field :name,

   225→      <div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-3">
   226→        <button type="button" onclick="document.getElementById('snap-modal-<%= sandbox.id %>').classList.add('hidden')"
   227→                class="px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900">Cancel</button>
   228→        <%= f.submit "Create Snapshot", class: "px-4 py-1.5 bg-blue-600 text-white text-sm font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 cursor-pointer" %>
   229→      </div>
   230→    <% end %>
   231→  </div>
   232→</div>
